 3329 |       const sd = effSD(s.key, p);
 3330 |       return { x: p.x, y: p.y, w: sd && sd > 0 ? 1 / (sd * sd) : 1 };

 3340 |     if (!fit || !fit.f) return;
 3341 |     const inner = { ...(plot.manualSD[s.key] || {}) };
 3342 |     includedPts(s).forEach((p) => {
 3343 |       if (!Number.isFinite(p.x)) return;
 3344 |       inner[p.instId] = Math.ceil((Math.abs(p.y - fit.f(p.x)) * 1.02 + 0.01) * 100) / 100;
 3345 |     });
 3346 |     set({ manualSD: { ...plot.manualSD, [s.key]: inner } });

 3348 |   const resetSD = (sKey) => { const next = { ...plot.manualSD }; delete next[sKey]; set({ manualSD: next }); };
 3349 |   const cleanOutliers = (target) => {
 3350 |     const thresh = parseManual(plot.outlierThreshStr) || 2.5;
 3351 |     const excluded = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(plot.excluded || {}));
 3352 |     const proc = (s) => {
 3353 |       for (let it = 0; it < 20; it++) {
 3354 |         const pts = s.pts.filter((p) => !((excluded[s.key] || {})[p.instId]) && Number.isFinite(p.x));
 3355 |         if (pts.length < 4) break;
 3356 |         const wpts = pts.map((p) => {
 3357 |           const sd = effSD(s.key, p);
 3358 |           return { x: p.x, y: p.y, w: sd && sd > 0 ? 1 / (sd * sd) : 1, p };
 3359 |         });
 3360 |         const fit = runFitModel(plot.fitModel, plot.customExpr, wpts);
 3361 |         if (!fit || !fit.f) break;
 3362 |         let worst = null, maxR = 0;
 3363 |         wpts.forEach((q) => {
 3364 |           const sd = effSD(s.key, q.p);
 3365 |           const err = sd && sd > 0 ? sd : 1;
 3366 |           const ratio = Math.abs(q.p.y - fit.f(q.p.x)) / err;
 3367 |           if (ratio > thresh && ratio > maxR) { maxR = ratio; worst = q.p; }
 3368 |         });
 3369 |         if (!worst) break;
 3370 |         excluded[s.key] = { ...(excluded[s.key] || {}), [worst.instId]: true };
 3371 |       }

 3374 |     set({ excluded });

 3378 |   // ===== Experimental-condition compatibility warnings =====
 3379 |   // When plotting Y as a function of the chosen variable field(s), every OTHER
 3380 |   // experimental condition should be held constant across the instances being
 3381 |   // compared. If it isn't (e.g. plotting vs temperature & pH while concentration
 3382 |   // silently differs between spectra), the fit/plot is scientifically misleading.
 3383 |   const conditionWarnings = useMemo(() => {
 3384 |     const includedInstIds = new Set();
 3385 |     series.forEach((s) => s.pts.forEach((p) => { if (!p.excluded) includedInstIds.add(p.instId); }));
 3386 |     const relevant = d.instances.filter((inst) => used[inst.id] !== false && includedInstIds.has(inst.id));
 3387 |     if (relevant.length < 2) return [];
 3388 |     const issues = [];
 3389 | 
 3390 |     // Variable field(s): flag inconsistent units (e.g. one spectrum's temperature
 3391 |     // in degC, another in K) — the x-values would not be directly comparable.
 3392 |     effXFields.forEach((fk) => {
 3393 |       const f = CD_EXPERIMENTAL_FIELDS.find((x) => x.key === fk);
 3394 |       if (!f || !f.unitKey) return;
 3395 |       const withUnit = relevant
 3396 |         .map((inst) => ({ inst, unit: getExpUnit(inst, f) || f.units[0] }))
 3397 |         .filter((v) => getExpValue(v.inst, f.key) !== '');
 3398 |       const unitSet = new Set(withUnit.map((v) => v.unit));
 3399 |       if (unitSet.size > 1) {
 3400 |         const majorityUnit = [...unitSet].sort((a, b) =>
 3401 |           withUnit.filter((v) => v.unit === b).length - withUnit.filter((v) => v.unit === a).length
 3402 |         )[0];
 3403 |         issues.push({
 3404 |           field: f,
 3405 |           isUnitMismatch: true,
 3406 |           refValue: majorityUnit,
 3407 |           offenders: withUnit.filter((v) => v.unit !== majorityUnit).map((v) => ({ instId: v.inst.id, name: v.inst.name, value: v.unit }))
 3408 |         });
 3409 |       }
 3410 |     });
 3411 | 
 3412 |     // Other (non-variable) fields: flag differing values across the compared spectra.
 3413 |     const others = CD_EXPERIMENTAL_FIELDS.filter((f) => !effXFields.includes(f.key));
 3414 |     const norm = (raw) => { const n = parseManual(raw); return n !== null ? Math.round(n * 1e6) / 1e6 : String(raw).trim().toLowerCase(); };
 3415 |     others.forEach((f) => {
 3416 |       const valued = relevant
 3417 |         .map((inst) => ({ inst, raw: getExpValue(inst, f.key) }))
 3418 |         .filter((v) => v.raw !== '' && v.raw !== undefined && v.raw !== null);
 3419 |       if (valued.length < 2) return;
 3420 |       const counts = new Map();
 3421 |       valued.forEach((v) => { const k = norm(v.raw); counts.set(k, (counts.get(k) || 0) + 1); });
 3422 |       if (counts.size <= 1) return;
 3423 |       let refKey = null, refCount = -1;
 3424 |       counts.forEach((cnt, k) => { if (cnt > refCount) { refCount = cnt; refKey = k; } });
 3425 |       const refValue = valued.find((v) => norm(v.raw) === refKey)?.raw;
 3426 |       const offenders = valued.filter((v) => norm(v.raw) !== refKey).map((v) => ({ instId: v.inst.id, name: v.inst.name, value: v.raw }));
 3427 |       if (offenders.length) issues.push({ field: f, refValue, offenders });
 3428 |     });

 1384 |                 })
 1385 |             );
 1386 | 
 1387 |             if (pts.length > 0) {
 1388 |                 const minConc = Math.min(...pts.map((p) => p.conc));
 1389 |                 const lowPts = pts.filter((p) => p.conc === minConc);
 1390 |                 lowPts.forEach((p) => {
 1391 |                     sumOD += p.od;
 1392 |                     count++;
 1393 |                 });
 1394 |             }
 1395 |         });
 1396 | 
 1397 |         if (count > 0) updatePlate({ ctrlType: 'none', ctrlODStr: (sumOD / count).toFixed(4) });
 1398 |     };
 1399 | 
 1400 |     const processedByRegion = useMemo(() => {
 1401 |         const byReg = {};
 1402 | 
 1403 |         if (plateType === '9x9box') return {};
 1404 | 
 1405 |         (grid || []).forEach((row, r) =>
 1406 |             (row || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1407 |                 if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;
 1408 |                 const reg = cellConfig[r]?.[c]?.region || 'Primary';
 1409 |                 if (!byReg[reg]) byReg[reg] = [];
 1410 |             })
 1411 |         );
 1412 | 
 1413 |         const result = {};
 1414 | 
 1415 |         Object.keys(byReg).forEach((reg) => {
 1416 |             const comps = plotCmps
 1417 |                 .map((name) => {
 1418 |                     const pts = [];
 1419 | 
 1420 |                     (grid || []).forEach((row, r) =>
 1421 |                         (row || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1422 |                             if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;
 1423 |                             const cfg = cellConfig[r]?.[c];
 1424 |                             if (!cfg) return;
 1425 |                             if ((cfg.region || 'Primary') !== reg || getRole(r, c) !== name) return;
 1426 | 
 1427 |                             const n = rawOD(r, c);
 1428 |                             if (isNaN(n)) return;
 1429 | 
 1430 |                             const xc = concOf(r, c, name);
 1431 |                             if (!(xc > 0)) return;
 1432 | 
 1433 |                             pts.push({
 1434 |                                 realX: xc,
 1435 |                                 logX: Math.log10(xc),
 1436 |                                 val: viability(n),
 1437 |                                 excl: cfg.excluded,
 1438 |                                 r,
 1439 |                                 c
 1440 |                             });
 1441 |                         })
 1442 |                     );
 1443 | 
 1444 |                     const byX = {};
 1445 | 
 1446 |                     pts.forEach((p) => {
 1447 |                         const k = concKey(p.realX);
 1448 |                         if (!byX[k]) byX[k] = { inc: [], excl: [] };
 1449 |                         (p.excl ? byX[k].excl : byX[k].inc).push(p);
 1450 |                     });
 1451 | 
 1452 |                     const vPts = [];
 1453 |                     const ePts = [];
 1454 | 
 1455 |                     Object.keys(byX).forEach((ks) => {
 1456 |                         const g = byX[ks];
 1457 |                         if (g.inc.length === 0 && g.excl.length === 0) return;
 1458 | 
 1459 |                         const rep = g.inc[0] || g.excl[0];
 1460 |                         const xr = rep.realX;
 1461 |                         const xl = rep.logX;
 1462 | 
 1463 |                         if (g.inc.length > 0) {
 1464 |                             const mean = g.inc.reduce((s, p) => s + p.val, 0) / g.inc.length;
 1465 |                             let sd = 0;
 1466 | 
 1467 |                             if (useFixedSD) sd = fSD;
 1468 |                             else if (g.inc.length > 1) {
 1469 |                                 sd = Math.sqrt(
 1470 |                                     g.inc.reduce((s, p) => s + Math.pow(p.val - mean, 2), 0) /
 1471 |                                         (g.inc.length - 1)
 1472 |                                 );
 1473 |                             }
 1474 | 
 1475 |                             vPts.push({
 1476 |                                 x: xl,
 1477 |                                 realX: xr,
 1478 |                                 y: mean,
 1479 |                                 sd: effectiveSD(name, xr, sd),
 1480 |                                 sdRaw: sd,
 1481 |                                 pts: g.inc
 1482 |                             });
 1483 |                         }
 1484 | 
 1485 |                         if (g.excl.length > 0) {
 1486 |                             ePts.push({
 1487 |                                 x: xl,
 1488 |                                 realX: xr,
 1489 |                                 y: g.excl.reduce((s, p) => s + p.val, 0) / g.excl.length,
 1490 |                                 pts: g.excl
 1491 |                             });
 1492 |                         }
 1493 |                     });
 1494 | 
 1495 |                     vPts.sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x);
 1496 |                     ePts.sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x);
 1497 | 
 1498 |                     if (vPts.length === 0 && ePts.length === 0) return null;
 1499 | 
 1500 |                     const color = cmpColor(name, allCmpds.indexOf(name));
 1501 |                     let fit = null;
 1502 | 
 1503 |                     if (fitIC50 && vPts.length >= 3) {
 1504 |                         let sumW = 0;
 1505 |                         const fitData = vPts.map((p) => {
 1506 |                             const w = p.sd > 0 ? 1 / (p.sd * p.sd) : 1;
 1507 |                             sumW += w;
 1508 |                             return {
 1509 |                                 x: p.realX,
 1510 |                                 y: p.y,
 1511 |                                 w,
 1512 |                                 sd: p.sd
 1513 |                             };
 1514 |                         });
 1515 | 
 1516 |                         if (sumW > 0) fitData.forEach((p) => (p.w = (p.w / sumW) * fitData.length));
 1517 |                         fit = fit4PL(fitData);
 1518 |                     }
 1519 | 
 1520 |                     return { name, color, vPts, ePts, fit };
 1521 |                 })
 1522 |                 .filter(Boolean);
 1523 | 
 1524 |             if (comps.length > 0) result[reg] = comps;
 1525 |         });
 1526 | 
 1527 |         return result;
 1528 |     }, [
 1529 |         grid,
 1530 |         cellConfig,
 1531 |         activePlateDim,
 1532 |         fitIC50,
 1533 |         useFixedSD,
 1534 |         fSD,
 1535 |         plotCmps,
 1536 |         allCmpds,
 1537 |         plateType
 1538 |     ]);
 1539 | 
 1540 |     const model = useMemo(
 1541 |         () => ({
 1542 |             processedByRegion,
 1543 |             plotCmps,
 1544 |             bgOD,
 1545 |             eScale,
 1546 |             activePlateDim,
 1547 |             unit
 1548 |         }),
 1549 |         [processedByRegion, plotCmps, bgOD, eScale, activePlateDim, unit]
 1550 |     );
 1551 | 
 1552 |     useEffect(() => {
 1553 |         if (plateModelRef) plateModelRef.current = model;
 1554 |     }, [model, plateModelRef]);
 1555 | 
 1556 |     const autoTouchSD = (region, name) => {
 1557 |         const compData = (processedByRegion[region] || []).find((c) => c.name === name);
 1558 |         if (!compData || !compData.fit) return;
 1559 | 
 1560 |         let maxRes = 0;
 1561 | 
 1562 |         compData.vPts.forEach((pt) => {
 1563 |             const yFit = 100 / (1 + Math.pow(pt.realX / compData.fit.ic50, compData.fit.hill));
 1564 |             const res = Math.abs(pt.y - yFit);
 1565 |             if (res > maxRes) maxRes = res;
 1566 |         });
 1567 | 
 1568 |         const newSD = Math.ceil((maxRes * 1.02 + 0.01) * 100) / 100;
 1569 |         const inner = { ...(manualErrors[name] || {}) };
 1570 | 
 1571 |         compData.vPts.forEach((pt) => {
 1572 |             inner[concKey(pt.realX)] = newSD;
 1573 |         });
 1574 | 
 1575 |         updatePlate({ manualErrors: { ...manualErrors, [name]: inner } });
 1576 |     };
 1577 | 
 1578 |     const autoTouchAll = () => {
 1579 |         let updates = {};
 1580 | 
 1581 |         Object.entries(processedByRegion).forEach(([, comps]) => {
 1582 |             comps.forEach((c) => {
 1583 |                 if (!c.fit) return;
 1584 | 
 1585 |                 let maxRes = 0;
 1586 | 
 1587 |                 c.vPts.forEach((pt) => {
 1588 |                     const yFit = 100 / (1 + Math.pow(pt.realX / c.fit.ic50, c.fit.hill));
 1589 |                     const res = Math.abs(pt.y - yFit);
 1590 |                     if (res > maxRes) maxRes = res;
 1591 |                 });
 1592 | 
 1593 |                 const newSD = Math.ceil((maxRes * 1.02 + 0.01) * 100) / 100;
 1594 | 
 1595 |                 if (!updates[c.name]) updates[c.name] = {};
 1596 | 
 1597 |                 c.vPts.forEach((pt) => {
 1598 |                     updates[c.name][concKey(pt.realX)] = newSD;
 1599 |                 });
 1600 |             });
 1601 |         });
 1602 | 
 1603 |         const next = { ...manualErrors };
 1604 | 
 1605 |         Object.keys(updates).forEach((cmp) => {
 1606 |             next[cmp] = { ...(next[cmp] || {}), ...updates[cmp] };
 1607 |         });
 1608 | 
 1609 |         updatePlate({ manualErrors: next, useFixedSD: true });
 1610 |     };
 1611 | 
 1612 |     const revertNormalSD = () => updatePlate({ manualErrors: {}, useFixedSD: false });

 1614 |     const updateCell = (r, c, v) => {
 1615 |         const ng = grid.map((row) => [...row]);
 1616 |         ng[r][c] = v;
 1617 |         updatePlate({ grid: ng });
 1618 |     };

 1620 |     const updateCmp = (c, v) => {
 1621 |         const role = v;
 1622 |         const nc = cellConfig.map((row) => row.map((cell) => ({ ...cell })));
 1623 |         const oldRole = compounds[c];
 1624 |         const isCtrl = role && ['cells', 'medium', 'pbs'].includes(role.toLowerCase());
 1625 | 
 1626 |         if (!role) {
 1627 |             for (let r = 0; r < activePlateDim.rows; r++) {
 1628 |                 if (nc[r][c].role === oldRole) {
 1629 |                     nc[r][c].role = null;
 1630 |                     nc[r][c].conc = null;
 1631 |                 }
 1632 |             }
 1633 |         } else {
 1634 |             let step = 0;
 1635 |             for (let r = 0; r < activePlateDim.rows; r++) {
 1636 |                 if (!nc[r][c].role || nc[r][c].role === oldRole) {
 1637 |                     nc[r][c].role = role;
 1638 |                     if (isCtrl) nc[r][c].conc = 0;
 1639 |                     else {
 1640 |                         const s = customConc[role] || { top: tConc, dil: dFact };
 1641 |                         nc[r][c].conc = s.top / Math.pow(s.dil, step);
 1642 |                     }
 1643 |                     step++;
 1644 |                 }
 1645 |             }
 1646 |         }
 1647 | 
 1648 |         const ncc = [...compounds];
 1649 |         ncc[c] = role;
 1650 |         updatePlate({ cellConfig: nc, compounds: ncc });
 1651 | 
 1652 |         const t = (v || '').trim();
 1653 |         if (t && !allCmpds.includes(t) && setCustomCmpds) {
 1654 |             setCustomCmpds((p) => [...(Array.isArray(p) ? p : []), t]);
 1655 |         }
 1656 |     };
 1657 | 
 1658 |     const updateRowCmp = (r, v) => {
 1659 |         const role = v;
 1660 |         const nc = cellConfig.map((row) => row.map((cell) => ({ ...cell })));
 1661 |         const oldRole = rowCompounds[r];
 1662 |         const isCtrl = role && ['cells', 'medium', 'pbs'].includes(role.toLowerCase());
 1663 | 
 1664 |         if (!role) {
 1665 |             for (let c = 0; c < activePlateDim.cols; c++) {
 1666 |                 if (nc[r][c].role === oldRole) {
 1667 |                     nc[r][c].role = null;
 1668 |                     nc[r][c].conc = null;
 1669 |                 }
 1670 |             }
 1671 |         } else {
 1672 |             let step = 0;
 1673 |             for (let c = 0; c < activePlateDim.cols; c++) {
 1674 |                 if (!nc[r][c].role || nc[r][c].role === oldRole) {
 1675 |                     nc[r][c].role = role;
 1676 |                     if (isCtrl) nc[r][c].conc = 0;
 1677 |                     else {
 1678 |                         const s = customConc[role] || { top: tConc, dil: dFact };
 1679 |                         nc[r][c].conc = s.top / Math.pow(s.dil, step);
 1680 |                     }
 1681 |                     step++;
 1682 |                 }
 1683 |             }
 1684 |         }

 1688 |         updatePlate({ cellConfig: nc, rowCompounds: nrc });
 1689 |

 1691 |         if (t && !allCmpds.includes(t) && setCustomCmpds) {
 1692 |             setCustomCmpds((p) => [...(Array.isArray(p) ? p : []), t]);

 1700 |     };

 1702 |     // Fill every well of the current selection with a preset label (cells /
 1703 |     // PBS / DMSO / Medium / empty) and NO concentration.
 1704 |     const patchSelectionRole = (label) => {
 1705 |         if (!activeSel) return;
 1706 |         const nc = cellConfig.map((row) => row.map((cell) => ({ ...cell })));
 1707 |         for (let r = activeSel.minR; r <= activeSel.maxR; r++) {
 1708 |             for (let c = activeSel.minC; c <= activeSel.maxC; c++) {

 1710 |             }
 1711 |         }

 1714 | 
 1715 |     const cleanOutliers = (targetReg = null, targetCmp = null) => {
 1716 |         const nc = cellConfig.map((row) => row.map((c) => ({ ...c })));
 1717 |         let changed = false;
 1718 | 
 1719 |         const processCmp = (reg, cmp) => {
 1720 |             for (let it = 0; it < 20; it++) {
 1721 |                 const pts = [];
 1722 |                 const byC = {};
 1723 | 
 1724 |                 (grid || []).forEach((_, r) =>
 1725 |                     (grid[r] || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1726 |                         if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;
 1727 |                         const cfg = nc[r][c];
 1728 |                         const role = getRole(r, c);
 1729 | 
 1730 |                         if (
 1731 |                             (cfg.region || 'Primary') === reg &&
 1732 |                             role === cmp.name &&
 1733 |                             !cfg.manualOverride &&
 1734 |                             !cfg.excluded
 1735 |                         ) {
 1736 |                             const n = rawOD(r, c);
 1737 |                             const xc = concOf(r, c, role);
 1738 | 
 1739 |                             if (!isNaN(n) && xc > 0) {
 1740 |                                 const val = viability(n);
 1741 |                                 const k = concKey(xc);
 1742 |                                 if (!byC[k]) byC[k] = [];
 1743 |                                 byC[k].push({
 1744 |                                     r,
 1745 |                                     c,
 1746 |                                     x: xc,
 1747 |                                     y: val,
 1748 |                                     realX: xc,
 1749 |                                     val
 1750 |                                 });
 1751 |                             }
 1752 |                         }
 1753 |                     })
 1754 |                 );
 1755 | 
 1756 |                 Object.values(byC).forEach((cps) => {
 1757 |                     const mean = cps.reduce((s, p) => s + p.y, 0) / cps.length;
 1758 |                     let sd = 0;
 1759 | 
 1760 |                     if (useFixedSD) sd = fSD;
 1761 |                     else if (cps.length > 1) {
 1762 |                         sd = Math.sqrt(cps.reduce((s, p) => s + Math.pow(p.y - mean, 2), 0) / (cps.length - 1));
 1763 |                     }
 1764 | 
 1765 |                     cps.forEach((p) => {
 1766 |                         p.computedSD = sd;
 1767 |                         p.effectiveSD = effectiveSD(cmp.name, p.realX, sd);
 1768 |                         pts.push(p);
 1769 |                     });
 1770 |                 });
 1771 | 
 1772 |                 if (pts.length < 4) break;
 1773 | 
 1774 |                 const ft = fit4PL(pts);
 1775 |                 if (!ft) break;
 1776 | 
 1777 |                 let worst = null;
 1778 |                 let maxRatio = 0;
 1779 | 
 1780 |                 pts.forEach((p) => {
 1781 |                     const pred = 100 / (1 + Math.pow(p.realX / ft.ic50, ft.hill));
 1782 |                     const residual = Math.abs(p.y - pred);
 1783 |                     const err = p.effectiveSD > 0 ? p.effectiveSD : fSD > 0 ? fSD : 1;
 1784 |                     const ratio = residual / err;
 1785 | 
 1786 |                     if (ratio > outlierThresh) {
 1787 |                         if (ratio > maxRatio) {
 1788 |                             maxRatio = ratio;
 1789 |                             worst = p;
 1790 |                         }
 1791 |                     }
 1792 |                 });
 1793 | 
 1794 |                 if (worst) {
 1795 |                     nc[worst.r][worst.c].excluded = true;
 1796 |                     changed = true;
 1797 |                 } else {
 1798 |                     break;
 1799 |                 }
 1800 |             }
 1801 |         };
 1802 | 
 1803 |         if (targetReg && targetCmp) {
 1804 |             processCmp(targetReg, { name: targetCmp });
 1805 |         } else {
 1806 |             Object.entries(processedByRegion).forEach(([reg, comps]) => {
 1807 |                 comps.forEach((cmp) => processCmp(reg, cmp));
 1808 |             });
 1809 |         }
 1810 | 
 1811 |         if (changed) updatePlate({ cellConfig: nc });
 1812 |     };

 1097 |     const getRole = (r, c) => {
 1098 |         const cfg = cellConfig[r]?.[c];
 1099 |         if (!cfg) return null;
 1100 |         if (cfg.role !== null && cfg.role !== undefined) return cfg.role;

 1103 |         if (rCmp && !cCmp) return rCmp;

 1300 |         if (!st || st.min === st.max) return { bg: base, dark: needsDarkText(base) };

 1715 |     const cleanOutliers = (targetReg = null, targetCmp = null) => {
 1716 |         const nc = cellConfig.map((row) => row.map((c) => ({ ...c })));
 1717 |         let changed = false;
 1718 | 
 1719 |         const processCmp = (reg, cmp) => {
 1720 |             for (let it = 0; it < 20; it++) {
 1721 |                 const pts = [];
 1722 |                 const byC = {};
 1723 | 
 1724 |                 (grid || []).forEach((_, r) =>
 1725 |                     (grid[r] || []).forEach((_, c) => {
 1726 |                         if (r >= activePlateDim.rows || c >= activePlateDim.cols) return;
 1727 |                         const cfg = nc[r][c];
 1728 |                         const role = getRole(r, c);
 1729 | 
 1730 |                         if (
 1731 |                             (cfg.region || 'Primary') === reg &&
 1732 |                             role === cmp.name &&
 1733 |                             !cfg.manualOverride &&
 1734 |                             !cfg.excluded
 1735 |                         ) {
 1736 |                             const n = rawOD(r, c);
 1737 |                             const xc = concOf(r, c, role);
 1738 | 
 1739 |                             if (!isNaN(n) && xc > 0) {
 1740 |                                 const val = viability(n);
 1741 |                                 const k = concKey(xc);
 1742 |                                 if (!byC[k]) byC[k] = [];
 1743 |                                 byC[k].push({
 1744 |                                     r,
 1745 |                                     c,
 1746 |                                     x: xc,
 1747 |                                     y: val,
 1748 |                                     realX: xc,
 1749 |                                     val
 1750 |                                 });
 1751 |                             }
 1752 |                         }
 1753 |                     })
 1754 |                 );
 1755 | 
 1756 |                 Object.values(byC).forEach((cps) => {
 1757 |                     const mean = cps.reduce((s, p) => s + p.y, 0) / cps.length;
 1758 |                     let sd = 0;
 1759 | 
 1760 |                     if (useFixedSD) sd = fSD;
 1761 |                     else if (cps.length > 1) {
 1762 |                         sd = Math.sqrt(cps.reduce((s, p) => s + Math.pow(p.y - mean, 2), 0) / (cps.length - 1));
 1763 |                     }
 1764 | 
 1765 |                     cps.forEach((p) => {
 1766 |                         p.computedSD = sd;
 1767 |                         p.effectiveSD = effectiveSD(cmp.name, p.realX, sd);
 1768 |                         pts.push(p);
 1769 |                     });
 1770 |                 });
 1771 | 
 1772 |                 if (pts.length < 4) break;
 1773 | 
 1774 |                 const ft = fit4PL(pts);
 1775 |                 if (!ft) break;

 1777 |                 let worst = null;
 1778 |                 let maxRatio = 0;
 1779 | 
 1780 |                 pts.forEach((p) => {
 1781 |                     const pred = 100 / (1 + Math.pow(p.realX / ft.ic50, ft.hill));
 1782 |                     const residual = Math.abs(p.y - pred);
 1783 |                     const err = p.effectiveSD > 0 ? p.effectiveSD : fSD > 0 ? fSD : 1;
 1784 |                     const ratio = residual / err;
 1785 | 
 1786 |                     if (ratio > outlierThresh) {
 1787 |                         if (ratio > maxRatio) {
 1788 |                             maxRatio = ratio;
 1789 |                             worst = p;
 1790 |                         }
 1791 |                     }
 1792 |                 });
 1793 | 
 1794 |                 if (worst) {
 1795 |                     nc[worst.r][worst.c].excluded = true;
 1796 |                     changed = true;
 1797 |                 } else {
 1798 |                     break;
 1799 |                 }

 1801 |         };

 2691 |         if (pts.length < 4) break;

 2693 |           const sd = effSD(s.key, p);

 2697 |         if (!fit || !fit.f) break;

 2715 |   // When plotting Y as a function of the chosen variable field(s), every OTHER
